<script type="text/javascript">
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
	    if (ContextHub && ContextHub.eventing) {
	        ContextHub.eventing.on(ContextHub.Constants.EVENT_TEASER_LOADED, function(event, data) {
                // Correct the markup structure for targeted Components.
                var refreshTargetingComponents = function(targetingId) {
                    var $targetedComponent = $('#' + targetingId).closest('.target');
                    // Do not modify DOM if component is an 'application-accordion'.
                    var isApplicationAccordion = $targetedComponent.find('.application-accordion').length > 0;
                    if (!isApplicationAccordion && $targetedComponent.find('.redesign').children().length > 0) {
                        var contentCont = $targetedComponent.find('.redesign').children();
                        // Save which elements should remain hidden after targeting.
                        var visibilityMapping = {};
                        contentCont.each(function(index, elem) {
                            var $elem = $(elem);
                            var visibilityObj = {
                                targetingObjIndex: index,
                                targetingObjContent: $elem.get(0),
                                isHidden: $elem.is(':hidden')
                            };
                            visibilityMapping['targetingObj_' + index] = visibilityObj;
                        });
                        var campaignCont = $targetedComponent.find('.campaign');
                        var redesignCont = $targetedComponent.find('.redesign');
                        var aemGridCont = $targetedComponent.find('.aem-Grid');
                        // Collect CSS classes for styling/JS logic.
                        var componentCSSClasses = redesignCont.attr('class');
                        // Remove obsolete DIV containers.
                        redesignCont.remove();
                        aemGridCont.remove();
                        // Move content and CSS classes at the right place.
                        $targetedComponent.addClass(componentCSSClasses);
                        $targetedComponent.find('.campaign').prepend(contentCont).attr('id', targetingId);
                        // Special handling for teaser with respect to different view ports.
                        contentCont.each(function(index, elem) {
                            var $contentElem = $(elem);
                            if (!visibilityMapping['targetingObj_' + index].isHidden) {
                                // Handle issue with 'teaser' components in IE.
                                if (ui.lib.isInternetExplorer() && $targetedComponent.hasClass('teaser')) {
                                    var contentElemCSSClasses = $contentElem.attr('class');
                                    $contentElem.closest('.campaign').addClass(contentElemCSSClasses);
                                    $contentElem.removeAttr('class');
                                }
                                // Smooth show - same as aem - kernel.js.
                                $contentElem.fadeIn();
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }
                // Handle visibility of targeted components which are configured to be hidden in certain experiences.
                var handleVisibility = function(targetingId) {
                    var $targetedComponent = $('#' + targetingId).closest('.target');
                    ui.log('Handle visibility for component with ID: ', targetingId);
                    $targetedComponent.toggleClass('hide', $targetedComponent.has('div.hidecomponent').length > 0);
                };
                if (dhl && dhl.lib && !dhl.lib.isAuthorInstance()) {
                    // Handle markup changes from targeting for each component individually (but only on publish).
                    var targetingId = "_content_de_de_privatkunden_jcr_content_par_responsivegrid_643857243_responsivepar_article";
                    var targetedElements = $(data.data);
                    targetedElements.each(function () {
                        if (targetingId && targetingId === this.key) {
                            handleVisibility(targetingId);
                            refreshTargetingComponents(targetingId);
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
	    }
	});
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
	    if (ContextHub && ContextHub.eventing) {
	        ContextHub.eventing.on(ContextHub.Constants.EVENT_TEASER_LOADED, function(event, data) {
                // Correct the markup structure for targeted Components.
                var refreshTargetingComponents = function(targetingId) {
                    var $targetedComponent = $('#' + targetingId).closest('.target');
                    // Do not modify DOM if component is an 'application-accordion'.
                    var isApplicationAccordion = $targetedComponent.find('.application-accordion').length > 0;
                    if (!isApplicationAccordion && $targetedComponent.find('.redesign').children().length > 0) {
                        var contentCont = $targetedComponent.find('.redesign').children();
                        // Save which elements should remain hidden after targeting.
                        var visibilityMapping = {};
                        contentCont.each(function(index, elem) {
                            var $elem = $(elem);
                            var visibilityObj = {
                                targetingObjIndex: index,
                                targetingObjContent: $elem.get(0),
                                isHidden: $elem.is(':hidden')
                            };
                            visibilityMapping['targetingObj_' + index] = visibilityObj;
                        });
                        var campaignCont = $targetedComponent.find('.campaign');
                        var redesignCont = $targetedComponent.find('.redesign');
                        var aemGridCont = $targetedComponent.find('.aem-Grid');
                        // Collect CSS classes for styling/JS logic.
                        var componentCSSClasses = redesignCont.attr('class');
                        // Remove obsolete DIV containers.
                        redesignCont.remove();
                        aemGridCont.remove();
                        // Move content and CSS classes at the right place.
                        $targetedComponent.addClass(componentCSSClasses);
                        $targetedComponent.find('.campaign').prepend(contentCont).attr('id', targetingId);
                        // Special handling for teaser with respect to different view ports.
                        contentCont.each(function(index, elem) {
                            var $contentElem = $(elem);
                            if (!visibilityMapping['targetingObj_' + index].isHidden) {
                                // Handle issue with 'teaser' components in IE.
                                if (ui.lib.isInternetExplorer() && $targetedComponent.hasClass('teaser')) {
                                    var contentElemCSSClasses = $contentElem.attr('class');
                                    $contentElem.closest('.campaign').addClass(contentElemCSSClasses);
                                    $contentElem.removeAttr('class');
                                }
                                // Smooth show - same as aem - kernel.js.
                                $contentElem.fadeIn();
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }
                // Handle visibility of targeted components which are configured to be hidden in certain experiences.
                var handleVisibility = function(targetingId) {
                    var $targetedComponent = $('#' + targetingId).closest('.target');
                    ui.log('Handle visibility for component with ID: ', targetingId);
                    $targetedComponent.toggleClass('hide', $targetedComponent.has('div.hidecomponent').length > 0);
                };
                if (dhl && dhl.lib && !dhl.lib.isAuthorInstance()) {
                    // Handle markup changes from targeting for each component individually (but only on publish).
                    var targetingId = "_content_de_de_privatkunden_jcr_content_par_responsivegrid_344831652_responsivepar_article";
                    var targetedElements = $(data.data);
                    targetedElements.each(function () {
                        if (targetingId && targetingId === this.key) {
                            handleVisibility(targetingId);
                            refreshTargetingComponents(targetingId);
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
	    }
	});
</script>

<div class="responsivegrid dhl redesign section">
<div class="container">
<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12">
<div class="target parbase aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12 article dhl redesign section">
<script type="text/javascript">
	document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
	    if (ContextHub && ContextHub.eventing) {
	        ContextHub.eventing.on(ContextHub.Constants.EVENT_TEASER_LOADED, function(event, data) {
                // Correct the markup structure for targeted Components.
                var refreshTargetingComponents = function(targetingId) {
                    var $targetedComponent = $('#' + targetingId).closest('.target');
                    // Do not modify DOM if component is an 'application-accordion'.
                    var isApplicationAccordion = $targetedComponent.find('.application-accordion').length > 0;
                    if (!isApplicationAccordion && $targetedComponent.find('.redesign').children().length > 0) {
                        var contentCont = $targetedComponent.find('.redesign').children();
                        // Save which elements should remain hidden after targeting.
                        var visibilityMapping = {};
                        contentCont.each(function(index, elem) {
                            var $elem = $(elem);
                            var visibilityObj = {
                                targetingObjIndex: index,
                                targetingObjContent: $elem.get(0),
                                isHidden: $elem.is(':hidden')
                            };
                            visibilityMapping['targetingObj_' + index] = visibilityObj;
                        });
                        var campaignCont = $targetedComponent.find('.campaign');
                        var redesignCont = $targetedComponent.find('.redesign');
                        var aemGridCont = $targetedComponent.find('.aem-Grid');
                        // Collect CSS classes for styling/JS logic.
                        var componentCSSClasses = redesignCont.attr('class');
                        // Remove obsolete DIV containers.
                        redesignCont.remove();
                        aemGridCont.remove();
                        // Move content and CSS classes at the right place.
                        $targetedComponent.addClass(componentCSSClasses);
                        $targetedComponent.find('.campaign').prepend(contentCont).attr('id', targetingId);
                        // Special handling for teaser with respect to different view ports.
                        contentCont.each(function(index, elem) {
                            var $contentElem = $(elem);
                            if (!visibilityMapping['targetingObj_' + index].isHidden) {
                                // Handle issue with 'teaser' components in IE.
                                if (ui.lib.isInternetExplorer() && $targetedComponent.hasClass('teaser')) {
                                    var contentElemCSSClasses = $contentElem.attr('class');
                                    $contentElem.closest('.campaign').addClass(contentElemCSSClasses);
                                    $contentElem.removeAttr('class');
                                }
                                // Smooth show - same as aem - kernel.js.
                                $contentElem.fadeIn();
                            }
                        });
                    }
                }
                // Handle visibility of targeted components which are configured to be hidden in certain experiences.
                var handleVisibility = function(targetingId) {
                    var $targetedComponent = $('#' + targetingId).closest('.target');
                    ui.log('Handle visibility for component with ID: ', targetingId);
                    $targetedComponent.toggleClass('hide', $targetedComponent.has('div.hidecomponent').length > 0);
                };
                if (dhl && dhl.lib && !dhl.lib.isAuthorInstance()) {
                    // Handle markup changes from targeting for each component individually (but only on publish).
                    var targetingId = "_content_de_de_privatkunden_jcr_content_par_responsivegrid_933937411_responsivepar_article";
                    var targetedElements = $(data.data);
                    targetedElements.each(function () {
                        if (targetingId && targetingId === this.key) {
                            handleVisibility(targetingId);
                            refreshTargetingComponents(targetingId);
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
	    }
	});
</script>

                document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
                    if (window.ContextHub && ContextHub.SegmentEngine) {

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
        ui.sub('login.changed', function(e, data) {
            if (dhl.sso.getAuthenticationLevel() > 2) {
                var nTimer = setInterval(function() {
                    if (window.$) {
                        $.mms.stage.prototype._getStartPosition = function(stageItem) {
                            if (stageItem.length > 2) {
                                return 1;
                            }
                        }
                        if ($('.owl-carousel').length) {
                            var owl = $('.owl-carousel');
                            owl.trigger('to.owl.carousel',1);
                            owl.trigger('stop.owl.autoplay');
                            clearInterval(nTimer);
                        }
                    }
                }, 100);
            } else {
                if ($('.owl-carousel').length) {
                    var owl = $('.owl-carousel');
                    owl.trigger('play.owl.autoplay');
                }
            }
        });
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {	
        ui.sub('cookie.setState', function(e, data) {
            if (dhl.sso.getAuthenticationLevel() > 2 && data.name == 'dhlolc' ) {
                var state = ui.hashCode(dhl.tracking.pageName);
                if (state = data.state) {
                    var states =  {};
                    states[state] = (Math.round(Date.now()/1000) + 365*24*60*60)*1;
                    setTimeout(function(){
                        ui.pub('cookie.set', {
                            name: 'dhlolc',
                            value: JSON.stringify(states),
                            days: 365
                        });
                    }, 100);
                }
            }
        });
		
		ui.sub('login.changed', function(e, data) {
            if (dhl.sso.getAuthenticationLevel() > 1) {
                $('.dhl.redesign.overlayarea.open.pagebottom').css( "display", "block");
            } else {
				$('.dhl.redesign.overlayarea.open.pagebottom').css( "display", "none");
			}
        });
    });
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function(event) {
		var wasTriggered = false;
		function openLogin() {
			if (eauth && !wasTriggered && location.hash.indexOf('#meineSendungen') >= 0) {
				wasTriggered = true;
				try {
					eauth.ensureAuthentication(function(){ location.hash = "#"; wasTriggered = false; }, 2);
				} catch (err) {
					ui.log("couldn't call ensureAuthentication");
				}
			}
		}
		ui.sub('login.changed', function(e,data){
			openLogin();
		});
		
		if (window.location.hash.indexOf('#meineSendungen') >= 0) {
			var oTimer = setInterval(function() {
				if (eauth) {
						openLogin();
						clearInterval(oTimer);
				}
			}, 100);
			
		}
		
		window.onhashchange = openLogin;
    });
</script>
</div>

<span id="eauth-sdk-2fa-activation-textbase">Schützen Sie Ihr DHL Kundenkonto mit einer zusätzlichen Maßnahme: Bei der Anmeldung über einen neuen Browser, müssen Sie sowohl Ihr Passwort, als auch einen Anmeldecode eingeben, der an Ihre E-Mail-Adresse gesendet wurde.</span>
</div>
